240: function serialToDate(value) {
241:   const serial = Number(value);
242:   if (!Number.isFinite(serial) || serial < 20000 || serial > 80000) return "";
243:   const utc = new Date(Math.round((serial - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));
244:   return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
245: }
246: 
247: function localDate(value) {
248:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
249:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
250:     return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
251:   const serialDate = serialToDate(value);
252:   if (serialDate) return serialDate;
253:   const text = String(value).trim();
254:   const match = text.match(/(\d{4})[.\-/년]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\-/월]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
255:   if (match) return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${match[3].padStart(2, "0")}`;
256:   const parsedText = new Date(text);
257:   if (!Number.isNaN(parsedText.getTime()) && /GMT|UTC|^[A-Za-z]{3}/.test(text))
258:     return `${parsedText.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(parsedText.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsedText.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
259:   const compact=text.replace(/\D/g,'');
260:   if(/^20\d{6}$/.test(compact))return compact.slice(0,4)+'-'+compact.slice(4,6)+'-'+compact.slice(6,8);
261:   const monthDay=text.match(/(?:^|\D)(\d{1,2})\s*(?:월|[.\-/])\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*일)?(?:\D|$)/);
262:   if(monthDay)return '2026-'+monthDay[1].padStart(2,'0')+'-'+monthDay[2].padStart(2,'0');
263:   return text;
264: }
265: 
266: function localTime(value) {
267:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
268:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
269:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;

4155: async function subjectConfidentialDigest(value){const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(String(value||''));const hash=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');}

1002:   const signal=student.supportSignal||{level:'green',summary:'별도 확인사항이 없습니다.'};
1003:   document.getElementById('studentSignalLayer')?.remove();
1004:   const level=signal.level||'green';
1005:   const title=level==='red'?'지도·상담 전 확인':(level==='yellow'?'지원정보 확인':'확인 완료');
1006:   const generic=level==='red'
1007:     ?'지도·상담 전에 확인해야 할 보호정보가 있습니다.'
1008:     :(level==='yellow'?'학생 지원과 관련해 확인할 정보가 있습니다.':'현재 별도 확인이 필요한 지원·보호 정보가 없습니다.');
1009:   const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='studentSignalLayer';layer.className='issue-layer student-signal-layer';
1010:   layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog student-signal-dialog">
1011:     <header><div><small>STUDENT SIGNAL</small><h3>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(student.name||'')}</h3><p>학생 선택 시 표시되는 지원·보호 확인 신호입니다.</p></div><button type="button" data-signal-close>×</button></header>
1012:     <div class="student-signal-summary signal-${level}"><i></i><div><b>${title}</b><p>${escapeHtml(generic)}</p></div></div>
1013:     <div class="student-signal-privacy"><b>민감정보 보호</b><p>이 화면에서는 상세 진단·가구자격·검사·상담 내용을 표시하지 않습니다. 필요한 경우 아래 버튼을 눌러 한 번 더 확인하세요.</p></div>
1014:     <div id="studentSignalDetails" class="student-signal-details hidden"></div>
1015:     <div class="modal-actions">${level!=='green'?'<button class="btn primary" data-signal-reveal>상세정보 확인</button>':''}<button class="btn secondary" data-signal-close>닫기</button></div>
1016:   </div>`;
1017:   document.body.appendChild(layer);
1018:   const close=()=>layer.remove();
1019:   layer.querySelectorAll('[data-signal-close]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=close);
1020:   layer.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===layer)close();};
1021:   layer.querySelector('[data-signal-reveal]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{
1022:     if(!canRevealStudentProtection())return toast('담임·학년부·관리자 권한에서 확인할 수 있습니다.');
1023:     if(!(await unlockSensitiveInfo()))return;
1024:     const details=layer.querySelector('#studentSignalDetails');const cards=[];
1025:     const field=(label,value)=>String(value||'').trim()?`<div class="student-signal-field"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><b>${escapeHtml(String(value))}</b></div>`:'';
1026: 
1027:     if(signal.educationSupport){
1028:       const x=signal.educationSupport;
1029:       cards.push(`<article><small>지원정보</small><h4>교육비지원</h4>
1030:         ${field('선정여부',x.selected)}
1031:         ${field('학생자격',x.studentQualification)}
1032:         ${field('가구자격',x.householdQualification)}
1033:         ${field('중위소득',x.incomePercent?`${x.incomePercent}%`:'')}
1034:         ${field('기준일',x.baseDate)}
1035:       </article>`);
--- student-signal @ 1009 ---
989: 
990: function updateStudentSignalIndicator(){
991:   const button=document.getElementById('studentSignalButton'); if(!button)return;
992:   const student=currentSignalStudent();
993:   if(!student){button.classList.add('hidden');button.classList.remove('signal-green','signal-yellow','signal-red');return;}
994:   const level=student.supportSignal?.level||'green';
995:   button.classList.remove('hidden','signal-green','signal-yellow','signal-red');button.classList.add(`signal-${level}`);
996:   const label=level==='red'?'확인 필요':(level==='yellow'?'지원 확인':'확인 완료');
997:   button.innerHTML=`<i></i><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(student.name||'')}</b><span>${label}</span>`;
998:   button.title=`${student.studentNo||''} ${student.name||''} · ${student.supportSignal?.summary||'별도 확인사항 없음'}`;
999: }

945: async function setSensitivePasswordFlow(){
946:   if(!canRevealStudentProtection())return toast('관리자·학년부장 권한에서 설정할 수 있습니다.');

950:   if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
951:     return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
952:   }
953:   const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
954:   localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));
955:   lockSensitiveSession();
956:   toast('민감정보 열람 비밀번호를 저장했습니다.');

4153: function subjectConfidentialPasswordHash(){return String(localStorage.getItem(UEP_SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_KEY)||'');}
4154: function subjectConfidentialPasswordConfigured(){return !!subjectConfidentialPasswordHash();}
4155: async function subjectConfidentialDigest(value){const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(String(value||''));const hash=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');}

8626: function normalizeTimetableGradeClass(row){
8627:   const directGrade=Number(row?.grade??row?.학년??0);
8628:   const directClass=Number(row?.classNo??row?.class??row?.반??0);
8629:   if(directGrade>=1&&directGrade<=3&&directClass>=1&&directClass<=20)return {grade:directGrade,classNo:directClass};
8630:   const raw=String(row?.classCode??row?.classroom??row?.학급??row?.반코드??row?.['학년반']??'').trim();
8631:   const digits=raw.replace(/\D/g,'');
8632:   if(digits.length===3){
8633:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1));
8634:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
8635:   }
8636:   if(digits.length===4){
8637:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1,3));
8638:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
8639:   }
8640:   return {grade:directGrade||1,classNo:directClass||Number(digits)||0};
8641: }

573: async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
574:   if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
575:   const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
576:   const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
577:   let changed=false, failed=false;
578:   for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
579:     if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
580:     const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
581:     if(!program?.source) continue;
582:     try{
583:       const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
584:       if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
585:       if(result.data) readonlyCache=result.data;
586:       const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
587:       if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];
588:       changed=true;
589:     }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
590:   }
591:   if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
592:   if(changed) await save();
593: }

887: async function sensitiveHash(value){
888:   const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
889:   const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
890:   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
891: }

9472: function studentAccessContext(student){
9473:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
9474:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9475:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");

9477:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
9478:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
9479:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
9480:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
9481:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact:!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||isOwnHomeroom)};
9482: }
